[E14 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__confluence_get_page id=KhfSbXfv] {"page_id": "page_qa_runbook"}
[E15 MAIN RESULT id=KhfSbXfv] {"id": "page_qa_runbook", "space_id": "space_ENG", "parent_id": "page_eng_home", "title": "QA Regression Runbook", "body": "<p>Manual QA regression pass run against every release candidate before we submit a build for review. Owned by QA (Vincent Parker); anyone cutting a build can run it, but a green matrix here is a gate on the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:content-title=\"Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission\"/><ac:link-body>Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission</ac:link-body></ac:link>. Scope covers Domino Delights (internal codename GoD, \"Game of Dominoes\"), Zombie Match 3D (listed as \"Puzzles vs Zombies\" until the July 2025 rename). Budget a few hours per title - the first five chapters alone run over an hour; if something fails, file the bug and block the release, don't hand-wave it.</p><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\"note\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Test the UPDATE path, not just a fresh install.</strong> The June 2025 progression losses were on <strong>Domino Delights</strong>, and nobody ever reproduced them - the leading hypothesis was the update path, which is why we test it. Every candidate must be installed <em>over</em> the last two shipped versions and checked for save/progression loss before we ship. If we only test fresh installs we will miss this entire class of bug again.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Recurring bug classes to always check</h2><p>These four have all bitten us in production. Treat them as non-negotiable checks on every pass, on real devices, not just the emulator.</p><ul><li><strong>Font / asset download stall on splash.</strong> In May 2025 Domino Delights froze at roughly 22% on the splash screen while pulling remote fonts and assets (ENG-2346, build 0.2.197). Watch the loading bar all the way to gameplay; test on a throttled/slow connection and with the app cache cleared.</li><li><strong>Lives &amp; scheduler regeneration.</strong> Confirm lives tick back up on the timer, refill correctly after device clock changes, and that offline time is credited without over-granting.</li><li><strong>Quest population &amp; reset.</strong> New quests populate on a fresh session, complete correctly, and roll over at the daily/weekly reset without duplicating or leaving empty slots.</li><li><strong>Save / progression integrity.</strong> Level, coins and unlocks survive an update, a force-quit and a re-launch. This is the one the June 2025 wipe belongs to.</li></ul><h2>Regression matrix</h2><p>Run each row on at least one iOS and one Android device. Both update rows are mandatory - N-1 and N-2 are the last two shipped store versions.</p><table><tbody><tr><th>Area</th><th>Fresh install</th><th>Update from N-1</th><th>Update from N-2</th><th>Notes</th></tr><tr><td>Splash / asset load</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Watch for the ~22% stall (May 2025 Domino Delights)</td></tr><tr><td>Save / progression</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>June 2025 wipe never reproduced in-house; update path is the leading hypothesis</td></tr><tr><td>Lives &amp; scheduler</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Test with device clock changes</td></tr><tr><td>Quests populate / reset</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Check daily + weekly rollover</td></tr><tr><td>Purchase / restore</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Sandbox account. NOTE: entitlements do <strong>not</strong> restore on reinstall and receipts are not validated server-side - see the per-title IAP row below. Check the purchase grants client-side, not that it restores.</td></tr></tbody></table><h3>If a check fails</h3><ul><li>File the bug with device, OS version, source build and repro steps, and link it here.</li><li>A failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release - fresh-install-only regressions do not pass the gate.</li><li>Player-facing symptoms (lost progress, stuck splash, missing lives) should be mirrored to the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:co
...[TRUNCATED 4371 chars]...
nt-gen iPhone, latest OS</td><td>iPhone SE-class, oldest supported OS</td><td>iPad (one gen)</td></tr>\n<tr><td>Android</td><td>current-gen Samsung / Pixel</td><td>low-RAM device, oldest supported OS</td><td>one Android tablet</td></tr></tbody></table>\n<h3>Sign-off</h3><p>QA posts the completed matrix in <code>#god-bugs (Domino Delights) or #zombie-bugs (ZM3D)</code> and links it from the release ticket. A build without a green matrix should not get submitted. In practice Friday is the usual release day and builds have shipped without a completed pass - QA has asked for a Friday freeze but it has not been adopted.</p>", "body_format": "storage", "status": "current", "version": 2, "author_id": "user_EMPLOYEE_0025", "created_at": "2024-04-15T10:11:23Z", "updated_at": "2025-08-04T10:00:00Z"}

[E27 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=3Cxotuxz] {"fileId": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59"}
[E28 MAIN RESULT id=3Cxotuxz] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# ZM submission rules\nLeonard, Sep 23\n\nAfter the orange juice one on Sunday I want the gate tighter, not just the Bug view in Linear.\n\nScope: every ZM ticket that's still open (any state but Done, Canceled or Duplicate), whatever team or label (ART or DES tickets with ZM in the title count too), plus everything that moved to Done after the last gate's builds were cut (Android #127 / iOS #40 on Sep 22). Release tickets, ZOM-359 and ZOM-475 stay out, that work is separate. From the next gate on, tickets filed while the gate is running get a row too, including the ones you file yourself.\n\nA ticket counts as fixed when Vincent or Brian saw it working on the candidate builds, or on prod if the fix is server side. A Done ticket for a bug nobody has re-checked yet also counts as fixed if its fix is actually in the candidate builds (or live on prod), noted as not re-checked. Done in Linear or a dev saying fixed isn't enough by itself, and if the latest check on the candidates still shows the bug, call it on how bad it is whatever the ticket says.\n\nBlocker = crash, freeze, player stuck or can't finish a level (like the orange juice one), lost progress / coins / lives (infinite-lives time included) / boosters / inventory items, or a purchase that doesn't deliver. Any other bug a player can see is a known issue, even if it doesn't change play, visual and sound glitches included. Art/VFX requests, requests for design, balance or tuning changes, analytics, tools, backend endpoints and new features aren't bugs, no call. The same goes for a ticket that only asks for new or updated art, animation, VFX, text style or UI layout (replacing an outdated model or icon with its new version included), even with a Bug label and even once it's Done, and for a ticket that doesn't say what goes wrong.\n\nBlockers go on the release ticket. If in doubt ask me before the go/no-go, not after.\n", "id": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59", "name": "ZM submission rules.docx"}

[E29 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=DxrEsfaG] {"fileId": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95"}
[E30 MAIN RESULT id=DxrEsfaG] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# How ZM submission builds are cut\nArthur, Sep 24\n\nSo nobody has to guess when gating:\n\n- Since the hotfix train ended on Sep 3, every store submission (the Match3D Android Submission and Match 3D iOS Submission jobs) is cut straight from main. The auth branch submission jobs are sign-in test builds, not store candidates.\n- A submission has exactly what was on main when its job started (the Started line in #builds) and nothing that landed after, even if the build finishes an hour later. Don't go by the Changes block either: the iOS posts only ever print the newest change, Android prints the whole list.\n- Backend is separate: match3d-backend changes reach players only when Douglas deploys the backend to prod, by hand (see the CI/CD page, CI stops at the build). Merged is not live.\n", "id": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95", "name": "How ZM submission builds are cut.docx"}

[E31 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=qzLVhquJ] {"fileId": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412"}
[E32 MAIN RESULT id=qzLVhquJ] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\nBrian Foster, Sep 30 2025 (after the fresh-install pass)\n\nUpdate rows from the QA regression runbook. Fresh-install rows are in my regression matrix sheet.\n\nDevice | OS | Installed from store | Updated to | Result | Notes\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-1 | iOS Submission #50 | FAIL | After the update the save came back at level 1 with 500 coins; the account was on level 212 with 18,400 coins before. Relaunching didn't bring it back.\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-2 | iOS Submission #50 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-1 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-2 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\n\nRepro for the iOS row: install the N-1 build from the App Store, play past level 200, install TestFlight 50 over it, launch.\n", "id": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412", "name": "Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135.docx"}

[E157 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_get_issue id=ExoWwiYJ] {"id": "ZOM-481"}
[E158 MAIN RESULT id=ExoWwiYJ] {"id": "ZOM-481", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-481", "number": 481, "title": "Zombie Health bar persists too long after death.", "description": "When the zombies die, their health bar is still present after a sec.  \n\nThe video below is in 0.3x. It shows that the health bar can clutter and cause visual noise. \n\nHowever, as you can notice in the video, it might also cause mistargets. If the items are matched fast enough, the first object matched after a zombie is killed can still target the dead zombie. Therefore, some items can be used for nothing which can also prevents balancing the board population. \n\n[persistent health bar - Made with Clipchamp.mp4]([URL_52796])", "priority": {"value": 1, "name": "Urgent"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-481/zombie-health-bar-persists-too-long-after-death", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-481-zombie-health-bar-persists-too-long-after-death", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T18:02:51Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-30T22:10:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Bug"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "owen.baker@harmonygames.co", "assigneeId": "usr_4e13bd40cc73d0fc", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 68459, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E188 MAIN RESULT id=MCqVn2PE] {"id": "ZOM-535", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-535", "number": 535, "title": "Fast matching might target an already-dead zombie", "description": "Here is a video demonstrating the difference between 75 CPM vs. 120 CPM simulation on the exact same level, because faster matchers end up with a lot of inefficiencies where their item doesn't deal damage since it's flying towards an already killed monster. As a result they end with the nuke. Meanwhile the 75 CPM average player ends up with quite a bit of items left. It's too big of a gap and we should really find ways to tighten all this up\n\n[[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6354].3602723.mp4]([URL_52842])\n\nHere is a slow-mo video of what happens. \n\n[persistent health bar - Made with Clipchamp (1).mp4]([URL_52841])", "priority": {"value": 1, "name": "Urgent"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-535/fast-matching-might-target-an-already-dead-zombie", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-535-fast-matching-might-target-an-already-dead-zombie", "createdAt": "2025-09-08T12:51:11Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-30T21:42:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Bug"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "EMPLOYEE_0032_NAME", "assigneeId": "usr_EMPLOYEE_0032", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 32770, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E268 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_get_issue id=yQ6AYWuu] {"id": "ZOM-237"}
[E269 MAIN RESULT id=yQ6AYWuu] {"id": "ZOM-237", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-237", "number": 237, "title": "packs animations bugs", "description": "[[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6254].0999997.mp4]([URL_52578])\n\nThe new banner animation plays twice per card. if you look for example at 00:23 when the card flips from the back to front, the new red banner its already on there and after it starts the animation again. \n\nI think we should move the banner and its pop up dotween animation into your card prefab and not let my card animation have it. I dont know how you controll the new banner to appear.\n\nSFX problems:\n\nBecause i dont know how you control the multiplier and new banner to appear when i added sfx to them the sfx still play in the background but if the banner doesnt exist or the multiplier text doesnt appear they still play in the background. Need a way we can add sfx to them things and play only when they appear.\n\nHighlight vfx on the cards:\n\n![image.png]([URL_52577])\n\nWe need highlight particles like we did with legendary cards for purple cards as well or perhaps the green one also we need to find out.\n\nRight now i also did for purple ones and its in the same folder, in the card prefab. they are: PurpleHighLight but they are curentley turned off.\n\n![image.png]([URL_52576])\n\nWe need to modify this script component somehow and we dont put the parent game object there but each individual vfx.\n\nSo for Legendary Particles should be LegendaryHighLight, for purple PurpleHighLight", "priority": {"value": 3, "name": "Medium"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-237/packs-animations-bugs", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-237-packs-animations-bugs", "createdAt": "2025-06-09T11:11:35Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-30T20:30:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Backlog", "statusType": "backlog", "labels": ["VFX", "Bug", "SFX"], "links": [], "attachments": [{"id": "6b156d6befed6e2f3d5f597a", "title": "ZOM-237__609ffa9f-a0d1-44.png", "subtitle": null, "url": "https://uploads.linear.app/a32d013e-0a11-5c58-ab4d-cd09016f571f/84f4e1b3-62c8-5515-9818-3283b745c090/ca2138ff-f91d-50d9-9ac9-806fe68ea2fa"}, {"id": "9a77d7c07351a645db1bdaf1", "title": "ZOM-237__024845ad-4b98-45.png", "subtitle": null, "url": "https://uploads.linear.app/a32d013e-0a11-5c58-ab4d-cd09016f571f/84f4e1b3-62c8-5515-9818-3283b745c090/a7f4d63b-45c4-542c-ab5a-a6d3f9946138"}], "createdBy": "marcus.bennett@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_c77c50cc15c5342d", "project": "Zombie Match 3D", "projectId": "dbab77c7-f871-4888-abf8-d98b52a41624", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 163910, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E391 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__confluence_search id=haRbanrn] {"text": "CI/CD"}
[E392 MAIN RESULT id=haRbanrn] {"total_count": 1, "limit": 25, "offset": 0, "has_more": false, "results": [{"id": "page_cicd_pipeline", "space_id": "space_ENG", "parent_id": "page_eng_home", "title": "CI/CD Pipeline & Branching", "body_format": "storage", "status": "current", "version": 2, "author_id": "user_EMPLOYEE_0031", "created_at": "2023-12-01T10:24:23Z", "updated_at": "2025-03-18T03:10:01Z", "body_length": 1858}]}

[E393 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__confluence_get_page id=VaJbiWuL] {"page_id": "page_cicd_pipeline"}
[E394 MAIN RESULT id=VaJbiWuL] {"id": "page_cicd_pipeline", "space_id": "space_ENG", "parent_id": "page_eng_home", "title": "CI/CD Pipeline & Branching", "body": "<p>How code gets from a pull request to production. In practice most work lands through long-lived feature branches rather than trunk-based development. Runtime gating is done with Firebase Remote Config (for example the A/B group assignment), not by branch strategy; deploys are rolling on ECS Fargate with health-check gating.</p><h2>Pipeline</h2><table><tbody><tr><th>Stage</th><th>What runs</th></tr><tr><td>PR</td><td>Lint, unit tests, build; required review before merge</td></tr><tr><td>Merge to main</td><td>The same <code>mix check</code> job runs again on push to main - there is no separate post-merge suite. <strong>CI stops here</strong> - the backend workflow (<code>match3d-backend/.github/workflows/check.yml</code>) runs compile and check on push/PR and has no deploy step.</td></tr><tr><td>Deploy</td><td><strong>Run by hand</strong>: rolling Fargate task-definition update via the <code>aws ecs update-service</code> command below; health checks gate promotion. The only CI-driven deploys in the org are the marketing site's S3 syncs (<code>website/.github/workflows/s3-deploy*.yml</code>).</td></tr><tr><td>Post-deploy</td><td>Smoke checks + alarm watch (see observability)</td></tr></tbody></table><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\"info\"><ac:rich-text-body><p>Client (Unity) builds are cut separately and go through staged store rollout - never 100% on day one. See the Ops release checklist.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Rollback</h2><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\"code\"><ac:plain-text-body><![CDATA[aws ecs update-service --cluster hg-prod --service game-api \\\n  --task-definition game-api:317 --force-new-deployment]]></ac:plain-text-body></ac:structured-macro><p>Infra + deploy targets: <ac:link><ri:page ri:content-title=\"AWS Infrastructure Guide\"/><ac:link-body>AWS Infrastructure Guide</ac:link-body></ac:link>.</p>", "body_format": "storage", "status": "current", "version": 2, "author_id": "user_EMPLOYEE_0031", "created_at": "2023-12-01T10:24:23Z", "updated_at": "2025-03-18T03:10:01Z"}

[E425 MAIN RESULT id=FKVF32Hs] {"id": "ZOM-477", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-477", "number": 477, "title": "DOT kills can take too long and cause perceived unresponsivenes", "description": "**When the kill cam is triggered, the game is slowed down. That causes some UX issues when zombies are killed by DOT, especially if they will be dead for the last damage by DOT.** \n\n[Record_[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6338].mp4]([URL_52793])\n\nThere are couple of ways to go around\n\n* If the kill cam is not triggered by an item thrown, don't make the sequence too slowed down. \n* Don't use more than 2 seconds of DOT\n* or implement the DOT death VFXs and make the death instant rather than counting down the life.", "priority": {"value": 3, "name": "Medium"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-477/dot-kills-can-take-too-long-and-cause-perceived-unresponsivenes", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-477-dot-kills-can-take-too-long-and-cause-perceived", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T11:51:12Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-24T09:20:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Improvement"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "EMPLOYEE_0032_NAME", "assigneeId": "usr_EMPLOYEE_0032", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 68833, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E445 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__slack_search_public_and_private id=LoMDJCgs] {"query": "level 1", "after": "1759190000", "limit": 20, "response_format": "concise"}
[E446 MAIN RESULT id=LoMDJCgs] {"ok": true, "messages": [{"ts": "1759274400.792657", "type": "message", "text": "checked the fixes since the sep 22 gate on #135 / #50: ZOM-503 giving lives ok, ZOM-506 zombie lane text ok, ZOM-557 consecutive login ok, ZOM-561 low-end black surfaces ok, ZOM-434 no flying items after backgrounding during load, ZOM-558 weekly board says Level: now. rest tomorrow", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0012_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1759248458.722989", "type": "message", "text": "Oh, I can see so many possibilities of cancer levels with those pigs and mixing mugs with the frizbees :[USERNAME_21162]:", "channel": "C08R8CHB0QL", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0005_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1759248458.722989"}, {"ts": "1759210834.849979", "type": "message", "text": "I just checked some folks that left level 6 but didn't start level 7, and you get these strange behavior that i'm 100% sure it's just bot.", "channel": "C090N54TG79", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1759207989.371869"}, {"ts": "1759210612.509939", "type": "message", "text": "so for all the people that didn't leave level 6, at least 11 are bots", "channel": "C090N54TG79", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1759207989.371869"}, {"ts": "1759210589.579679", "type": "message", "text": "I had to spot check just to see if bots go deeper into the funnel, so I extended this to level 6, so begin 1 but did not left 6. It increases the count by 3, and I checked all 3 logs, all bots...", "channel": "C090N54TG79", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1759207989.371869"}, {"ts": "1759210228.486879", "type": "message", "text": "Out of 28 that I observed that started level 1, but did not have a left level 5 event (basically all those who didn't make it past level 5)\n\nAT LEAST EIGHT are bots. These are the ones that I'm 99% sure. There are still a lot of heavily questionable ones but I wanted to be lenient and not just blame bots.\n\nFor example if a user loads the app, loads level 1, did not click on a single item, quits. I did not consider this a bot.", "channel": "C090N54TG79", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1759207989.371869"}], "has_more": false, "total_count": 6, "response_metadata": {"next_cursor": ""}}

Will LiveOps to addressables do the trick you think? Do we have to go after the objects and monsters?\n\nI should note that we currently don't have addressables in ZM. We will need to set up the addressable manager stuff from DD. So There is some more lifting than just making things into an addressable.", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0025_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1758905363.341129"}, {"ts": "1758903616.346579", "type": "message", "text": "After I skipped the zombie lane tutorial, ı got the exception and skipping couldn’t claim the ice cream. <@EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID>   ", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0012_SLACK_ID", "thread_ts": "1758903616.346579"}, {"ts": "1758827960.705049", "type": "message", "text": "and both colliders are equal", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758827946.430599", "type": "message", "text": "I was able to see this one in my first play", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758827933.392849", "type": "message", "text": "which may cause performance issues as the colliders are quite complex", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758827921.413599", "type": "message", "text": "looks like we can have objects that have more than 1 click collider", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758827895.596809", "type": "message", "text": "", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758823160.030389", "type": "message", "text": "It could very well be an OS bug that we can't fix.", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758823142.221059", "type": "message", "text": "I think on Samsung devices, when you trigger that prompt, or about to, it disables the audio, because the permission dialog is an OS window, and it's an equivalent of homing out. So the app thinks it's in the background, at least from an audio standpoint", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758823108.379949", "type": "message", "text": "OK Fortress Merge also has the weird sound bug. So I was playing, and suddenly my audio disappears... I don't know why, nothing happened on screen. Then I force quit and restart my app, the moment I restart, I get the Push notification permission prompt.", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0016_SLACK_ID"}, {"ts": "1758767858.307829", "type": "message", "text": "Yeah go for it. Once it’s merged we can trigger a testflight

k it's related to fix for the issue below because it's related to leaderboard.\n*Issue 1 <@EMPLOYEE_0003_SLACK_ID>* \n*`First[TSource]`  InvalidOperationException - Sequence contains no matching element <https://console.Firebase.google.com/project/zombie-match-3d/crashlytics/app/ios:com.calmplay.zombie/issues/959e6a349628f7c17d3fba7646a5128a?time=7d&amp;versions=0.3.38%20(38);0.3.36%20(36)&amp;sessionEventKey=e2695603faec42d9800207de2e093c9d_2129586384031167888|link>*\nCondition:\n• It happens *after lvl 2*\n• It happens when players are not in the weekly leaderboard\n• Peak at September 14th , after September 17th no issue\nAffected Players:\n• 22 events in 14 users", "channel": "C08R27X0EAH", "user": "EMPLOYEE_0012_SLACK_ID"}], "has_more": false, "response_metadata": {"next_cursor": ""}}

[E484 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_create_issue id=Xk21kBUy] {"team": "Zombie Match 3D", "title": "iOS #50 installed over the N-1 store build resets the save to level 1 (progress and coins lost)", "description": "Found on the update-path rows of the QA regression runbook during the ZOM-570 gate (Brian's update-path run for iOS #50 / Android #135).\n\n**Device:** iPhone 13, iOS 18.6\n**Source build:** N-1 store build (App Store) → **updated to:** iOS Submission #50 (TestFlight 50)\n\n**What happens:** after the update the save comes back at level 1 with 500 coins. The account was on level 212 with 18,400 coins before. Relaunching doesn't bring it back.\n\n**Repro:**\n1. Install the N-1 build from the App Store.\n2. Play past level 200.\n3. Install TestFlight 50 (iOS Submission #50) over it.\n4. Launch.\n\n**Expected:** level, coins and unlocks survive the update (runbook: Save / progression, Update from N-1).\n\n**Other update rows:** iOS N-2 → #50 pass; Android N-1 → #135 pass; Android N-2 → #135 pass.\n\nLost progress/coins = blocker under the ZM submission rules. Runbook: a failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release. Player-facing symptom (lost progress) should get a known-issue note in the Customer Support Playbook.\n\nSource: \"Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\" (Brian Foster, Sep 30).", "priority": 1, "labels": ["Bug"], "relatedTo": ["ZOM-570"]}

[E534 MAIN ASSISTANT-TEXT]
The QA call on Android #135 / iOS #50 is **NO-GO**, with 14 blockers. The register, the ZOM-570 description, the new ticket and the thread reply are all done.

**What I did**
- **Gate register:** added a new tab, "Sep 30 - Android #135 / iOS #50", to the ZM3D Release Gate sheet. It has 133 rows: 14 blockers, 22 known issues, 11 fixed and 86 no-call. It covers every open ZM ticket, including the ART and DES ones with ZM in the title, plus everything that moved to Done after the Sep 22 builds were cut. Release tickets, ZOM-359 and ZOM-475 are left out, per Leonard's rules.
- **New ticket, ZOM-581:** the runbook check that installs the candidate over the last shipped version (N-1) failed on iOS. Installing #50 over the N-1 App Store build reset the save to level 1 with 500 coins; it was on level 212 with 18,400 coins. Brian found this in his update run, but nobody had filed it. The other three update checks (iOS from N-2, Android from N-1 and N-2) passed. His "regression matrix green" only covers fresh installs, and a failed update check blocks the release on its own.
- **ZOM-570:** added the 14 blockers to the description by ticket key, reusing existing tickets.
- **Slack:** replied in Arthur's candidates thread in #zombie-bugs with the call.

**Blockers**
- **Crash, freeze or stuck:**
  - ZOM-573: claiming the 5th Collect&Win chest crashes the app.
  - ZOM-449: low-end Android freezes on a cold start into level 3.
  - ZOM-578: the tutorial gets stuck at step 3.
  - ZOM-554: after quitting mid-tornado, the next level can't be played until a force-quit.
  - ZOM-546: the endless board drains until the run can't go on.
  - ZOM-435: the trophy path still sticks on #135. ZOM-435 is marked Done, and I saw it pass on #133, but Leonard reproduced it on #135 last night.
  - DES-313: level 214 can't be finished because only 2 of 3 jars spawn.
  - ZOM-559: the Samsung victory screen froze. This was last seen on #133 and still needs a re-check on a Samsung with the candidates.
- **Lost items or a purchase that doesn't deliver:**
  - ZOM-483: new-install infinite-lives time is lost on relaunch.
  - ZOM-560: the abandoned pack doesn't grant the win streak freezer time.
  - ZOM-576: the Season Pass premium tier drops the booster bundle.
  - ZOM-581: the save reset described above.
- **Marked fixed, but not in the builds:**
  - ZOM-569: the ticket was closed at 18:05 on a branch fix. The fix itself (commit a61cb2b) only reached main at 21:36, after both jobs started at 18:43 and 18:45.
  - ZOM-567: the backend fix (match3d-backend #36) is deployed to prod, but the data load it depends on hasn't been run on prod. Arthur claimed a chest on prod and nothing landed. He's holding that load until after the store push, so ZOM-567 stays a blocker unless that changes.

**Calls you may want to check**
- **ZOM-563 marked fixed and live on prod:** this is based on your DM to Douglas at 22:58 saying it "works as expected". The ticket is still In Progress in Linear.
- **ZOM-562 (orphan items in the boss arena) called a known issue:** the comments don't say it stops a run.
- **ZOM-473 marked fixed but not re-checked:** it was closed before the builds were cut.

Before Friday, ZOM-559 needs that Samsung re-check, and ZOM-578 needs a QA reproduction because only Leonard has seen it so far.
